# Etherwise — Proof Points & Capabilities

_Compiled 2026-08-12 05:15 IST · 978 agency + 571 cross-client facts (1533 trusted)._

> Agency-level knowledge extracted from client calls but NOT specific to one client. Cited; use CONFIRMED / CROSS-VERIFIED for outreach.

## Agency capabilities & method

### architecture
- A browser/Chrome extension (one-click) is being used that adds tasks via API, attaches the email thread as a PDF, auto-generates a subject-based case name, and remembers the selected list until a different one is chosen. [CONFIRMED · fathom/163774252 @ 00:22:03 – 00:23:00]
- A client matching tool was built that uses notes, preferences, and AI-based best matching to return best and lowest matches for connecting clients with technicians/service providers. [CONFIRMED · fathom/156775218 @ 00:21:25]
- A dual-agent system is being designed: one agent writes prompts and a second agent verifies those prompts against established guidelines. [CONFIRMED · fathom/132222000 @ 00:03:00 - 'एक एजेंट जो प्रॉम्ट लिख रहा है और दूसरा एज़ेंट जो यह गाइड्लाइन्स के अगेंस वेरिफाय कर रहा है']
- A future enhancement discussed is a browser-native tool (not Gmail-specific) that can directly add emails or data from multiple sources (e.g., Gmail, Notion) into the tracking system. [CONFIRMED · fathom/163774252 @ 00:23:00]
- A mitigation strategy being adopted is to ask Claude at frequent intervals (after each logical unit of work, not every instruction) to list exactly which scripts or parts it edited, in order to catch unintended changes early. [CONFIRMED · fathom/110334517 @ 00:03:00 – 'we just, you know, there and there ask it for, okay, tell me the things that you edited or like, you know, scripts that you updated, like doing it more frequently']
- A settings table in the system stores a 'default program record ID' that is used to assign a default program to imported leads. [CONFIRMED · fathom/96863100 @ 00:02:37]
- A ticket submission form was updated to include an explanation of the priority system and expected resolution times: urgent = 1 day, medium = 3–5 days, low = longer window. [CONFIRMED · fathom/115914688 @ 00:01:10]
- A voice agent system is composed of three distinct AI model layers: speech-to-text, a text-to-text LLM (deciding what to say next), and text-to-speech — all combined into one pipeline. [CONFIRMED · fathom/99116769 @ 00:02:06]
- Abhi built a commission/salary calculation script in Airtable that computes closer bookings, applies commission percentages, calculates monthly salaries, and sends them out via email — with no third-party platform interaction. [CONFIRMED · fathom/132450295 @ there are things like calculate... how many bookings came in, what is the commission for each closer... calculate what salary is based on that... preparing the salaries and sending those out via email]
- Abhi built a script-based automation that listens for Stripe payment webhooks, then uses an e-signature API to populate and send contracts, and sends a follow-up email notification to the payer. [CONFIRMED · fathom/132450295 @ whenever there is a person who... makes a payment... we have a webbook that watches for like, you know, any Stripe... then we want to send them maybe the offer letter or the contract letter]
- Abhi implements Airtable automations including: Calendly webhook-to-Airtable record capture, interface button triggers (e.g., send offer letter, send contract), date-based email reminders, and Make.com scenarios for third-party integrations not natively supported in Airtable. [CONFIRMED · fathom/132450295 @ 00:06:35 - 00:08:02]
- Abhi recommends using Airtable custom interfaces (supporting Next.js/JavaScript code) as the primary layer team members interact with, keeping the underlying tables in the backend to avoid unauthorized direct edits. [CONFIRMED · fathom/166586786 @ 00:09:14]
- Abhi uses UML/entity relationship diagrams in draw.io to architect Airtable base structures (tables, link records, lookup fields, rollup fields) before implementation, and documents these for client review before building. [CONFIRMED · fathom/132450295 @ I always have that habit of like, you know, architecting stuff before implementing anything... I kind of use maybe a draw IO is the tool that I use a lot]
- Abhi's performance optimization approach for large Airtable bases includes offloading transactional or non-critical data to Google Sheets (linked via Airtable record ID) to stay within Airtable's 500,000 records per table limit. [CONFIRMED · fathom/132450295 @ on the Airtable baseline, there is like 500,000 records per table per base limit... why not create a Google Sheet with the Airtable record ID and maintain those details there?]
- Abhijeet (agency) recommended ManyChat instead of GoHighLevel for Instagram/TikTok DM automation, explaining GoHighLevel handles SMS and email well but not direct Instagram DM integration. ManyChat integrates with Make.com and Airtable for lead logging. [CONFIRMED · fathom/149957674 @ 00:15:22 – 'ManyChat is a really good tool when we want to communicate through social media platform... Airtable would be your database for the leads and then we'll use Make.com to glue ManyChat with the Airtable.']
- Abhijeet Gandhi noted that the planned power-dialer/transfer functionality is achievable with the voice agents already built by the agency. [CONFIRMED · fathom/100449673 @ 00:10:51]
- Abhijeet Gandhi prioritizes which processes to automate by calculating hours saved per team member per day and multiplying across the month (e.g. 5 people × 1 hour/day = 150 hours/month saved). [CONFIRMED · fathom/171448316 @ five people... putting in an hour daily doing certain tasks... five hours a day that is 150 hours that I'm going to save within a month]
- Abhijeet Gandhi's proposal methodology: 2-3 onboarding calls with the operational team to map current processes, followed by a detailed architecture diagram and documentation covering what to automate, the pipeline design, tooling, and estimated hours — all delivered before any build begins. [CONFIRMED · fathom/171448316 @ I usually have two to three onboarding calls with the actual team... then I go ahead and prepare the architecture diagram, the documentation... you have a complete plan before you say yes, or I start working on anything]
- Abhijeet advised against finalizing tools on the discovery call; the approach is to first understand all pain points and business processes, then recommend the right tools and architecture. [CONFIRMED · fathom/149957674 @ 00:17:00 – 'I'm not trying to finalize any tools in these calls, I would understand all the requirements much better.']
- Abhijeet explained that API-based integration is more reliable than UI scraping because the API remains stable even when the UI changes, whereas UI automation breaks on interface upgrades. [CONFIRMED · fathom/140347413 @ the API is always more reliable... because the API never changes even if the UI is changing]
- Abhijeet noted that Claude has native connectors to OneDrive, enabling it to access files stored there directly. [CONFIRMED · fathom/140347413 @ directly have connectors in Cloud to connect with OneDrive so you can access the information as it needs]
- Abhijeet proposed a normalization/fairness mechanism where instead of filling one client's bucket entirely before moving to the next, leads are distributed in small interleaved batches (e.g. 25 to client A, 25 to client B, repeat) to ensure all clients receive leads even if total volume is lower than expected. [CONFIRMED · fathom/155692198 @ 00:11:34]
- Abhijeet proposed that Claude could access the Roam database directly (bypassing the Roam UI/API) using database queries, since Roam is itself a UI layer over a database. [CONFIRMED · fathom/140347413 @ we can talk to the developer and just get access to the database... Claude can access directly the database and not having to deal with the Roam]
- Abhijeet proposed using unique UTM/query-parameter links per sales rep (e.g., biglabit.de/incorporation?ref=Felix) so that deals created through the website can be attributed to the correct sales rep without the customer noticing. [CONFIRMED · fathom/170920604 @ 00:22:34 – 00:23:10]
- Abhijeet recommended migrating automation from Google Apps Script to Zapier for better log visibility and easier debugging. [CONFIRMED · fathom/159234042 @ the only reason I would lean towards Zapier is just the visibility that we have over the logs... Zapier is like much easier visibility wise by looking at what went wrong.]

- After ATCC course completion, if certification is no experience: update active status to scheduling and notify Kiera for candidate to be scheduled [CONFIRMED · https://www.loom.com/share/3d84b3e929dd4e57bd0f68c4778dba10 @ if the candidate's certification is no experience, we update their active status to scheduling and notify Kiera that the candidate has completed their ATCC course and they can, like, be rescued. - Agency advises that Claude Chat's Projects feature can be used to upload MD and PRD files so they are accessible across multiple chat sessions without relying solely on conversation memory. [CONFIRMED · fathom/130269236 @ 00:10:10 - 'on the left panel, you will see there is projects... you ask the cloud chat to create multiple documents and add this to the memory and the files you see here']
- Agency advises that Claude Code has a context limit similar to Claude Chat; long single threads cause context loss, so milestone checkpoints should be written to MD files regularly. [CONFIRMED · fathom/130269236 @ 00:04:35 - 'Claude code has the context limit as well. So you cannot just keep going through one thread']
- Agency advises using Claude Code's plan mode for discussion and planning (no code execution), and edit/auto mode only when ready to implement changes. [CONFIRMED · fathom/130269236 @ 00:04:01 - 'there is a plan mode that is asked before editing mode... whenever you're planning something, you got to be in the plan mode']
- Agency approach to large PDF ingestion: chunk documents into smaller segments (e.g. 20 pages), process each chunk separately, then combine responses rather than feeding entire PDFs in one API call. [CONFIRMED · fathom/52352314 @ [00:07:10] Jay Pokharna: instead what we do is maybe break it down into the chunks of 20 pages and then share and then at the end combine all the responses into the one]
- Agency approach: keep client's existing workflow unchanged initially; integrate tools to read data, build dashboards, and establish metrics before layering in AI automation. [CONFIRMED · fathom/158862212 @ 00:01:41 — 'we want to keep your current workflow as is…we are just preparing some dashboards, some tracking']
- Agency capability: AI outbound calling with VAPI where, if the person answers, the AI speaks a brief qualifying line (e.g. 'is this the right time to speak?') and then transfers the live call to a human coordinator. [CONFIRMED · fathom/122372377 @ 00:05:55 – 'usually there is one or two lines that AI says... is it the right time to speak with you and I can connect you with the coordinator... And then if the person says yes, then we transfer it to the human']
- Agency cold email campaign strategy: send ~25 emails/day per email account; with 10 accounts that yields ~250 emails/day. Campaign platforms provide metrics on inbox delivery, open rates, and replies. [CONFIRMED · fathom/48149397 @ even if you send 25 emails a day, then we still have 10 email accounts so that will be 250 emails a day]
- Agency guideline: do not send more than 100 emails per day from a single warmed-up inbox; sending 1,000+ emails per day requires 9–10 separate warmed-up inboxes. [CONFIRMED · fathom/122372377 @ 00:14:25 – 'the limit is that you don't send more than a hundred a day from a single inbox... if you want to send out a thousand emails... you need nine to ten inboxes that are warmed up']
- Agency outreach campaigns automatically stop sending follow-ups to a prospect as soon as they reply, preventing duplicate or redundant automated messages. [CONFIRMED · fathom/48149397 @ these campaigns have ability to whenever they respond to us on an email, that campaign stops. So we don't have to worry that if someone has replied and still the automated email goes out]
- Agency proposes building a small Python app with a simple UI to consolidate all manual copy-paste steps into one interface, rather than using Make.com, because the workflow interacts with locally stored files that Make cannot access directly. [CONFIRMED · fathom/98414614 @ 00:08:43]
- Agency recommends creating persistent markdown/context files in the working directory to serve as a knowledge base for Claude Code, preventing context loss across sessions. Suggested folder name: 'context'. [CONFIRMED · fathom/130269236 @ 00:04:24 - 'you want to try and create as many files as you can within your working directory' and 00:13:06 - 'You can create like a folder called as context']
- Agency recommends narrowing the purpose of each AI model to a specific segment (e.g. 30 of 120 files per model) to maximise accuracy; AI performs better on fewer focused tasks. [CONFIRMED · fathom/52352314 @ [00:15:38] Jay Pokharna: the best thing with AI is it works really good when you try to narrow down its purpose... if you give it five things, it will do all the five things very good.]
- Agency recommends no more than two links in a cold outreach email to avoid spam flags. [CONFIRMED · fathom/48149397 @ it is usually recommended that we don't have more than two links in an email]
- Agency recommends that after each major milestone or checkpoint in Claude Code, the client should instruct Claude to write a summary document into the context folder so it can be referenced in future sessions. [CONFIRMED · fathom/130269236 @ 00:04:53 - 'whenever you feel like, okay, there was a big milestone or a big checkpoint that we discussed, just say, hey, can we write this down? into a doc']

- Agency's current automation approach is to automate processes as they currently exist first, then evaluate whether to consolidate everything into Airtable or HubSpot once the full system is understood. [CONFIRMED · fathom/168397077 @ 00:14:10]
- Agency's delivery methodology: architect the solution first using diagrams, define inputs/outputs for each automation, then hand implementation to the team; Abhijeet remains the single point of contact throughout. [CONFIRMED · fathom/138573079 @ 00:04:53]
- Agency's design principle: always pair automations with a manual button so the action can be triggered manually if the automation fails. [CONFIRMED · fathom/52523654 @ 00:17:40 - 'I like to have automated ways of doing it also a button so case automation doesn't work we can go manually click the button, that's what I like to have both']
- Agency's standard discovery method: ask the client to walk through their manual workflow step-by-step (or provide a workflow diagram) before attempting to design any automation. [CONFIRMED · fathom/56778897 @ 00:02:45]
- Agency's standard pre-implementation step: produce a workflow/architecture diagram showing which steps live in GoHighLevel vs. Make.com and what the updated GHL record looks like, get client sign-off, then implement — to avoid mid-build plan changes. [CONFIRMED · fathom/117799510 @ 00:11:09 – 'I'll give you a small architecture diagram or the workflow diagram… once you give me a go ahead, then I go and implement… we only spend like an hour before implementing anything']
- Agency's voice agent missed-call forwarding approach: configure call forwarding on the client's existing phone provider so unanswered calls forward to the Vapi agent number, allowing the client to retain their existing number. [CONFIRMED · fathom/56778897 @ 00:15:44]
- Airtable custom interfaces do not support mobile; clients using mobile must be informed and an alternative native interface must be built for mobile use cases. [CONFIRMED · fathom/163774252 @ 00:14:03]
- Airtable dashboard views can only display data from one row at a time; it is not possible to combine or visualize multiple rows together in a single dashboard view, which is why a row-per-milestone approach is required instead of a column-per-milestone approach. [CONFIRMED · fathom/128905225 @ 00:05:39]
- Airtable's native automations cannot use third-party JavaScript libraries, which was a known limitation discussed when considering shifting email workflows fully into Airtable. [CONFIRMED · fathom/163774252 @ 00:06:00]
- Airtable's native table view does not support Monday.com-style hierarchical dropdown nesting; the agency works around this by building a custom interface layer that shows expandable grouped views. - The long-form ad generation pipeline (built for Alpha Agents) works as follows: (1) copywriting phase produces a CSV with script, visual instructions, and character handle; (2) an orchestrator runs two steps — generate scene and generate ad; (3) an LLM converts script + visual instructions into structured scene output (location, camera, lighting, wardrobe, props, dialogue, mood, word count); (4) scenes are sent to Sora for video generation; (5) generated videos are transcribed with local Whisper; (6) an LLM compares transcript to expected dialogue, trims made-up words, and decides whether to regenerate; (7) scenes are stitched together and captions are added via Creatomate. [CONFIRMED · fathom/103440499 @ 00:07:46]
- The marketing metrics system uses an Airtable script to link appointment and cash flow records to a marketing matrix table by day.
